ॐ

Śrī Satyanārāyaṇa Vratakalpam

The observance of Satyanārāyaṇa · pūjā and the kathā of the Skānda Purāṇa

The whole vrata as kept at Annavaram: the preliminaries and resolve, Gaṇapati pūjā, the lokapālas, the nine planets and the guardians of the quarters, the sixteen-fold worship of Satyanārāyaṇa with the Puruṣa and Śrī Sūktas, and the five chapters of the vrata-kathā (Skānda Purāṇa, Revākhaṇḍa). The Sanskrit only is given; the ritual directions appear as short English notes between the texts. Vedic mantras are shown without accents.

all stotras · pronunciation guide

Pūrvāṅgam · the preliminaries ↑

The vrata may be kept on a full-moon day, an Ekādaśī, the day the sun enters a new sign, or any auspicious day, in the evening or the morning, after bathing and the daily rites. Clean the place of worship, draw a rangoli of rice-flour, turmeric and kuṅkuma, raise a small maṇḍapa hung with mango leaves, and light the lamp. Sit facing east or north. Devotion matters more than expense: do it as your means allow.

Husband and wife stand together holding betel leaves with a fruit (or a tender coconut) and bow.

oṃ devīṃ vācamajanayanta devāstāṃ viśvarūpāḥ paśavo vadanti |
sā no mandreṣamūrjaṃ duhānā dhenurvāgasmānupa suṣṭutaitu ||
ayaṃ muhūrtassumuhūrto'stu ||

word by word · translation

om om devīm divine vācam Speech ajanayanta brought forth devāḥ the gods tām her viśva-rūpāḥ of every form paśavaḥ creatures vadanti speak sā may she naḥ to us mandrā gladdening iṣam food ūrjam and strength duhānā yielding dhenuḥ the cow vāk Speech asmān to us upa near su-ṣṭutā well praised etu come ayam this muhūrtaḥ moment su-muhūrtaḥ a good moment astu may it be

The gods brought forth the goddess Speech; creatures of every form give voice to her. May she, delightful, yielding refreshment and strength like a milch-cow, come to us, well praised. May this hour be an auspicious hour.

The betel leaves are set down on plates.

yāḥ phalinīryā aphalā apuṣpā yāśca puṣpiṇīḥ |
bṛhaspatiprasūtāstā no muñcantvagṃhasaḥ ||

word by word · translation

yāḥ those that phalinīḥ bear fruit yāḥ those that aphalāḥ bear no fruit apuṣpāḥ those without flowers yāḥ those that ca and puṣpiṇīḥ bear flowers bṛhaspati-prasūtāḥ sent forth by Bṛhaspati tāḥ may they naḥ us muñcantu free aṃhasaḥ from distress

The plants that bear fruit and those that bear none, the flowerless and the flowering — sent forth by Bṛhaspati, may they free us from distress.

phalaṃ manorathaphalaṃ putrapautrapravardhanam |
yasmāttasmācchivaṃ me syādataśśāntiṃ prayaccha me ||
phaladānakalpoktasampūrṇasakalaphalāvāptirastu ||
manorathaphalasiddhirastu ||

word by word · translation

phalam a fruit manaḥ-ratha-phalam the fruit of the heart's desire putra-pautra-pravardhanam that increases sons and grandsons yasmāt since tasmāt therefore śivam good me to me syāt may come ataḥ so śāntim peace prayaccha grant me me phala-dāna-kalpa-ukta-sampūrṇa-sakala-phala-avāptiḥ the gaining of every fruit declared in the rite of giving fruit astu may there be manaḥ-ratha-phala-siddhiḥ fulfilment of the heart's desire astu may there be

This fruit is the fruit of the heart's wish, increasing sons and grandsons; so may it bring me good, and grant me peace. May every fruit promised for the gift of fruit be fully gained; may the heart's wish be fulfilled.

Sip water (ācamana). Light the lamp, offer akṣata and bow; then sip three times more from the spoon, reciting the twenty-four names.

Ācamanam · the twenty-four names ↑

oṃ keśavāya svāhā | oṃ nārāyaṇāya svāhā | oṃ mādhavāya svāhā |
oṃ govindāya namaḥ | oṃ viṣṇave namaḥ | oṃ madhusūdanāya namaḥ |
oṃ trivikramāya namaḥ | oṃ vāmanāya namaḥ | oṃ śrīdharāya namaḥ |
oṃ hṛṣīkeśāya namaḥ | oṃ padmanābhāya namaḥ | oṃ dāmodarāya namaḥ |
oṃ saṅkarṣaṇāya namaḥ | oṃ vāsudevāya namaḥ | oṃ pradyumnāya namaḥ |
oṃ aniruddhāya namaḥ | oṃ puruṣottamāya namaḥ | oṃ adhokṣajāya namaḥ |
oṃ nārasiṃhāya namaḥ | oṃ acyutāya namaḥ | oṃ janārdanāya namaḥ |
oṃ upendrāya namaḥ | oṃ haraye namaḥ | oṃ kṛṣṇāya namaḥ ||

word by word · translation

om keśavāya svāhā om, hail to Keśava om nārāyaṇāya svāhā om, hail to Nārāyaṇa om mādhavāya svāhā om, hail to Mādhava om govindāya namaḥ om, salutation to Govinda om viṣṇave namaḥ om, salutation to Viṣṇu om madhusūdanāya namaḥ om, salutation to Madhusūdana om trivikramāya namaḥ om, salutation to Trivikrama om vāmanāya namaḥ om, salutation to Vāmana om śrīdharāya namaḥ om, salutation to Śrīdhara om hṛṣīkeśāya namaḥ om, salutation to Hṛṣīkeśa om padmanābhāya namaḥ om, salutation to Padmanābha om dāmodarāya namaḥ om, salutation to Dāmodara om saṅkarṣaṇāya namaḥ om, salutation to Saṅkarṣaṇa om vāsudevāya namaḥ om, salutation to Vāsudeva om pradyumnāya namaḥ om, salutation to Pradyumna om aniruddhāya namaḥ om, salutation to Aniruddha om puruṣottamāya namaḥ om, salutation to Puruṣottama om adhokṣajāya namaḥ om, salutation to Adhokṣaja om nārasiṃhāya namaḥ om, salutation to Nārasiṃha om acyutāya namaḥ om, salutation to Acyuta om janārdanāya namaḥ om, salutation to Janārdana om upendrāya namaḥ om, salutation to Upendra om haraye namaḥ om, salutation to Hari om kṛṣṇāya namaḥ om, salutation to Kṛṣṇa

Water is sipped at each of the first three names (svāhā); the hands are washed at the next two; the rest are recited with salutation. Keśava, Nārāyaṇa, Mādhava, Govinda, Viṣṇu, Madhusūdana, Trivikrama, Vāmana, Śrīdhara, Hṛṣīkeśa, Padmanābha, Dāmodara, Saṅkarṣaṇa, Vāsudeva, Pradyumna, Aniruddha, Puruṣottama, Adhokṣaja, Nārasiṃha, Acyuta, Janārdana, Upendra, Hari, Kṛṣṇa — salutation to each.

Daiva prārthanā · prayers to the gods ↑

yaśśivo nāmarūpābhyāṃ yā devī sarvamaṅgalā |
tayossaṃsmaraṇātpuṃsāṃ sarvato jayamaṅgalam ||

word by word · translation

yaḥ he who is śivaḥ Śiva nāma-rūpābhyām in name and form yā she who is devī the Goddess sarva-maṅgalā Sarvamaṅgalā tayoḥ of those two saṃsmaraṇāt by the remembrance puṃsām for men sarvataḥ on every side jaya-maṅgalam there is victory and blessing

Śiva, known by name and form, and the Goddess who is all-auspicious: by remembering the two, people gain victory and blessing on every side.

lābhasteṣāṃ jayasteṣāṃ kutasteṣāṃ parābhavaḥ |
yeṣāmindīvaraśyāmo hṛdayastho janārdanaḥ ||

word by word · translation

lābhaḥ gain teṣām is theirs jayaḥ victory teṣām is theirs kutaḥ whence teṣām for them parābhavaḥ defeat yeṣām in whose indīvara-śyāmaḥ dark as the blue lotus hṛdaya-sthaḥ heart dwells janārdanaḥ Janārdana

Theirs is gain, theirs is victory — how could they be overcome, in whose hearts dwells Janārdana, dark as the blue lotus?

āpadāmapahartāraṃ dātāraṃ sarvasampadām |
lokābhirāmaṃ śrīrāmaṃ bhūyo bhūyo namāmyaham ||

word by word · translation

āpadām of troubles apahartāram the remover dātāram the giver sarva-sampadām of all prosperity loka-abhirāmam the delight of the world śrī-rāmam Śrī Rāma bhūyaḥ bhūyaḥ again and again namāmi bow to aham I

Again and again I bow to Śrī Rāma, remover of calamities, giver of all riches, the delight of the world.

sarvamaṅgalamāṅgalye śive sarvārthasādhike |
śaraṇye tryambake devi nārāyaṇi namo'stu te ||

word by word · translation

sarva-maṅgala-māṅgalye O auspiciousness of all that is auspicious śive O gracious one sarva-artha-sādhike who accomplish every purpose śaraṇye O refuge tri-ambake three-eyed devi O Goddess nārāyaṇi O Nārāyaṇī namaḥ salutation astu be te to you

O auspiciousness of all that is auspicious, gracious one, accomplisher of every aim, refuge, three-eyed Goddess Nārāyaṇī — salutation to you.

śrīlakṣmīnārāyaṇābhyāṃ namaḥ | umāmaheśvarābhyāṃ namaḥ |
vāṇīhiraṇyagarbhābhyāṃ namaḥ | śacīpurandarābhyāṃ namaḥ |
arundhatīvasiṣṭhābhyāṃ namaḥ | śrīsītārāmābhyāṃ namaḥ |
sarvebhyo mahājanebhyo namaḥ | ayaṃ muhūrtassumuhūrto'stu ||

word by word · translation

śrī-lakṣmī-nārāyaṇābhyām namaḥ salutation to Śrī Lakṣmī and Nārāyaṇa umā-maheśvarābhyām namaḥ salutation to Umā and Maheśvara vāṇī-hiraṇyagarbhābhyām namaḥ salutation to Sarasvatī and Brahmā śacī-purandarābhyām namaḥ salutation to Śacī and Indra arundhatī-vasiṣṭhābhyām namaḥ salutation to Arundhatī and Vasiṣṭha śrī-sītā-rāmābhyām namaḥ salutation to Śrī Sītā and Rāma sarvebhyaḥ to all mahā-janebhyaḥ the great ones namaḥ salutation ayam this muhūrtaḥ moment su-muhūrtaḥ a good moment astu may it be

Salutation to Lakṣmī and Nārāyaṇa, to Umā and Maheśvara, to Vāṇī and Brahmā, to Śacī and Indra, to Arundhatī and Vasiṣṭha, to Sītā and Rāma, and to all the great ones. May this hour be an auspicious hour.

Bhūtoccāṭanam — dispelling the spirits: sprinkle a few grains of akṣata behind you.

uttiṣṭhantu bhūtapiśācāḥ ete bhūmibhārakāḥ |
eteṣāmavirodhena brahmakarma samārabhe ||

word by word · translation

uttiṣṭhantu let them rise and go bhūta-piśācāḥ the ghosts and fiends ete these bhūmi-bhārakāḥ that burden the earth eteṣām of them avirodhena without the hindrance brahma-karma the sacred rite samārabhe I begin

Let the spirits and goblins rise and go, these burdens of the earth; without hindrance from them I begin this sacred rite.

Prāṇāyāma — restrain the breath while reciting:

oṃ bhūḥ | oṃ bhuvaḥ | ogṃ suvaḥ | oṃ mahaḥ | oṃ janaḥ | oṃ tapaḥ | ogṃ satyam |
oṃ tatsaviturvareṇyaṃ bhargo devasya dhīmahi | dhiyo yo naḥ pracodayāt ||
oṃ āpo jyotī raso'mṛtaṃ brahma bhūrbhuvassuvarom ||

word by word · translation

om bhūḥ om, earth om bhuvaḥ om, mid-region om suvaḥ om, heaven om mahaḥ om, the great world om janaḥ om, the world of creation om tapaḥ om, the world of austerity om satyam om, the world of truth om om tat that savituḥ of Savitṛ vareṇyam most excellent bhargaḥ radiance devasya of the god dhīmahi we meditate on dhiyaḥ thoughts yaḥ who naḥ our pracodayāt may impel om om āpaḥ the waters jyotiḥ light rasaḥ essence amṛtam nectar brahma Brahman bhūḥ bhuvaḥ suvaḥ om earth, mid-region, heaven, om

Oṃ — the seven worlds, from earth to the world of truth. We meditate on the adorable radiance of the divine Savitṛ; may he impel our minds. Oṃ — the waters, the light, the essence, the immortal, Brahman: earth, atmosphere, heaven, Oṃ.

Saṅkalpam · the resolve ↑

Holding akṣata and water, fill in the year, season, month, fortnight, tithi and weekday where the dots stand, and your gotra and name.

mamopāttaduritakṣayadvārā śrīparameśvaraprītyarthaṃ
śubhe śobhane muhūrte śrīmahāviṣṇorājñayā pravartamānasya
adya brahmaṇaḥ dvitīyaparārdhe śvetavarāhakalpe vaivasvatamanvantare
kaliyuge prathamapāde jambūdvīpe bharatavarṣe bharatakhaṇḍe
asmin vartamāne vyāvahārike cāndramānena … saṃvatsare … ayane
… māse … pakṣe … tithau … vāsare śubhanakṣatre śubhayoge śubhakaraṇe
evaṃ guṇaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭāyāṃ śubhatithau
śrīmān … gotraḥ … nāmadheyaḥ dharmapatnīsameto'haṃ
śrīmataḥ … gotrasya … nāmadheyasya dharmapatnīsametasya mama sakuṭumbasya
kṣemasthairyavijayābhayāyurārogyaiśvaryābhivṛddhyarthaṃ
dharmārthakāmamokṣacaturvidhapuruṣaphalāvāptyarthaṃ
cintitamanorathasiddhyarthaṃ śrīsatyanārāyaṇamuddiśya
śrīsatyanārāyaṇaprītyarthaṃ puruṣasūktavidhānena
dhyānāvāhanādiṣoḍaśopacārapūjāṃ kariṣye |
ādau nirvighnaparisamāptyarthaṃ gaṇādhipatipūjāṃ kariṣye |
tadaṅgakalaśārādhanaṃ kariṣye ||

word by word · translation

mama my upātta-durita-kṣaya-dvārā through the destruction of accumulated sins śrī-parama-īśvara-prīti-artham to please the supreme Lord śubhe at an auspicious śobhane fair muhūrte moment śrī-mahā-viṣṇoḥ of Śrī Mahāviṣṇu ājñayā by the command pravartamānasya now in course adya today brahmaṇaḥ of Brahmā dvitīya-para-ardhe in the second half of life śveta-varāha-kalpe in the aeon of the White Boar vaivasvata-manu-antare in the age of Manu Vaivasvata kali-yuge in the Kali age prathama-pāde in its first quarter jambū-dvīpe on the continent of Jambū bharata-varṣe in the land of Bharata bharata-khaṇḍe in the region of Bharata asmin in this vartamāne current vyāvahārike civil reckoning cāndra-mānena by the lunar measure saṃvatsare in the year named ayane in the half-year māse in the month pakṣe in the fortnight tithau on the lunar day vāsare on the weekday śubha-nakṣatre under an auspicious star śubha-yoge in an auspicious yoga śubha-karaṇe in an auspicious karaṇa evam thus guṇa-viśeṣaṇa-viśiṣṭāyām distinguished by these qualities śubha-tithau on this auspicious day śrīmān the fortunate gotraḥ of the lineage named nāmadheyaḥ bearing the name dharma-patnī-sametaḥ together with my lawful wife aham I śrīmataḥ of the fortunate gotrasya of the lineage named nāmadheyasya bearing the name dharma-patnī-sametasya together with his lawful wife mama of me sa-kuṭumbasya with my family kṣema-sthairya-vijaya-abhaya-āyuḥ-ārogya-aiśvarya-abhivṛddhi-artham for the increase of welfare, stability, victory, fearlessness, long life, health and prosperity dharma-artha-kāma-mokṣa-catuḥ-vidha-puruṣa-phala-avāpti-artham to gain the four aims of life: dharma, wealth, pleasure and liberation cintita-manaḥ-ratha-siddhi-artham for the fulfilment of the wishes in my mind śrī-satyanārāyaṇam Śrī Satyanārāyaṇa uddiśya with reference to śrī-satyanārāyaṇa-prīti-artham to please Śrī Satyanārāyaṇa puruṣa-sūkta-vidhānena by the rite of the Puruṣa Sūkta dhyāna-āvāhana-ādi-ṣoḍaśa-upacāra-pūjām the worship of sixteen services, beginning with meditation and invocation kariṣye I shall perform ādau first nirvighna-parisamāpti-artham for its completion without obstacle gaṇa-adhipati-pūjām the worship of Gaṇapati kariṣye I shall perform tat-aṅga-kalaśa-ārādhanam the worship of the water-vessel that is part of it kariṣye I shall perform

For the removal of the sins I have gathered and for the pleasure of the Supreme Lord — at this bright and auspicious hour, in the age set going by the command of Mahāviṣṇu, in the second half of Brahmā's life, in the Śvetavarāha kalpa, the manvantara of Vaivasvata, the first quarter of the Kali age, in Jambūdvīpa, in the land of Bharata; in the present year, half-year, month, fortnight, lunar day and weekday so named, under an auspicious star, yoga and karaṇa — I, of such a gotra and name, with my wife, for the safety, steadiness, victory, fearlessness, long life, health and increasing prosperity of myself and my family, for the four ends of life — dharma, wealth, desire and liberation — and for the fulfilment of the wishes I hold, will perform the sixteen-fold worship of Śrī Satyanārāyaṇa, from meditation and invocation onward, in the manner of the Puruṣa Sūkta, for his pleasure. First, that it may be completed without obstacle, I will worship Gaṇapati; and as part of it, worship the sacred vessel.

Kalaśārādhanam · worship of the vessel ↑

kalaśasya mukhe viṣṇuḥ kaṇṭhe rudrassamāśritaḥ |
mūle tatra sthito brahmā madhye mātṛgaṇāśritāḥ ||
kukṣau tu sāgarāssarve saptadvīpā vasundharā |
ṛgvedo'tha yajurvedassāmavedo hyatharvaṇaḥ |
aṅgaiśca sahitāssarve kalaśaṃ tu samāśritāḥ ||

word by word · translation

kalaśasya of the vessel mukhe at the mouth viṣṇuḥ Viṣṇu kaṇṭhe at the neck rudraḥ Rudra samāśritaḥ resides mūle at the base tatra there sthitaḥ stands brahmā Brahmā madhye in the middle mātṛ-gaṇa-āśritāḥ the hosts of Mothers reside kukṣau in its belly tu and sāgarāḥ the oceans sarve all sapta-dvīpā with its seven continents vasundharā the earth ṛk-vedaḥ the Ṛgveda atha and yajuḥ-vedaḥ the Yajurveda sāma-vedaḥ the Sāmaveda hi indeed atharvaṇaḥ the Atharvaveda aṅgaiḥ their auxiliaries ca and sahitāḥ together with sarve all kalaśam in the vessel tu indeed samāśritāḥ reside

At the mouth of the vessel dwells Viṣṇu, at its neck Rudra, at its base Brahmā, and in its middle the hosts of Mothers. In its belly are all the oceans and the earth with its seven continents; the Ṛg, Yajus, Sāma and Atharva Vedas with their limbs have all taken refuge in the vessel.

Put sandal-paste, flowers and akṣata into the vessel and cover its mouth with the right hand.

āpo vā idagṃ sarvaṃ viśvā bhūtānyāpaḥ prāṇā vā āpaḥ paśava
āpo'nnamāpo'mṛtamāpassamrāḍāpo virāḍāpassvarāḍāpaśchandāgṃsyāpo
jyotīgṃṣyāpo yajūgṃṣyāpassatyamāpassarvā devatā āpo bhūrbhuvassuvarāpa om ||

word by word · translation

āpaḥ the waters vai truly idam are this sarvam all viśvā all bhūtāni beings āpaḥ are the waters prāṇāḥ the life-breaths vai truly āpaḥ are the waters paśavaḥ cattle āpaḥ are the waters annam food āpaḥ is the waters amṛtam nectar āpaḥ is the waters samrāṭ the sovereign āpaḥ is the waters virāṭ the cosmic ruler āpaḥ is the waters svarāṭ the self-ruler āpaḥ is the waters chandāṃsi the metres āpaḥ are the waters jyotīṃṣi the lights āpaḥ are the waters yajūṃṣi the Yajus formulae āpaḥ are the waters satyam truth āpaḥ is the waters sarvāḥ all devatāḥ the deities āpaḥ are the waters bhūḥ bhuvaḥ suvaḥ earth, mid-region and heaven āpaḥ are the waters om om

The waters are all this. All beings are the waters; the waters are the breaths, the cattle, food, the immortal; the waters are sovereign, far-ruling, self-ruling; the metres, the lights, the sacrificial formulae, the truth, all the gods are the waters. Earth, atmosphere, heaven — the waters. Oṃ.

gaṅge ca yamune caiva godāvari sarasvati |
narmade sindhu kāveri jale'smin sannidhiṃ kuru ||
āyāntu śrīsatyanārāyaṇapūjārthaṃ duritakṣayakārakāḥ ||

word by word · translation

gaṅge O Gaṅgā ca and yamune O Yamunā ca and eva too godāvari O Godāvarī sarasvati O Sarasvatī narmade O Narmadā sindhu O Sindhu kāveri O Kāverī jale in water asmin this sannidhim your presence kuru make āyāntu may they come śrī-satyanārāyaṇa-pūjā-artham for the worship of Śrī Satyanārāyaṇa durita-kṣaya-kārakāḥ destroyers of sin

O Gaṅgā, Yamunā, Godāvarī, Sarasvatī, Narmadā, Sindhu and Kāverī, be present in this water. May they come, destroyers of sin, for the worship of Śrī Satyanārāyaṇa.

Kalaśodakena devam ātmānaṃ pūjādravyāṇi ca samprokṣya — with water from the vessel, sprinkle the deity, yourself and the articles of worship.

❧

Gaṇapati Pūjā ↑

gaṇānāṃ tvā gaṇapatigṃ havāmahe kaviṃ kavīnāmupamaśravastamam |
jyeṣṭharājaṃ brahmaṇāṃ brahmaṇaspata ā naśśṛṇvannūtibhissīda sādanam ||

word by word · translation

gaṇānām of the hosts tvā you gaṇa-patim the lord of hosts havāmahe we invoke kavim the seer kavīnām of seers upama-śravaḥ-tamam of highest renown jyeṣṭha-rājam the eldest king brahmaṇām of sacred words brahmaṇaḥ-pate O lord of the sacred word ā hither naḥ us śṛṇvan hearing ūtibhiḥ with your help sīda sit down sādanam on the seat

We call on you, lord of the hosts, poet among poets, of highest renown, eldest king of prayers, Brahmaṇaspati: hearing us, come with your help and sit in your seat.

śrīmahāgaṇādhipataye namaḥ | dhyāyāmi | āvāhayāmi | ratnasiṃhāsanaṃ samarpayāmi |
pādayoḥ pādyaṃ samarpayāmi | hastayorarghyaṃ samarpayāmi |
mukhe ācamanīyaṃ samarpayāmi ||

word by word · translation

śrī-mahā-gaṇa-adhipataye to the great Lord of hosts namaḥ salutation dhyāyāmi I meditate on him āvāhayāmi I invoke him ratna-siṃha-āsanam a jewelled lion-throne samarpayāmi I offer pādayoḥ at his feet pādyam water for the feet samarpayāmi I offer hastayoḥ in his hands arghyam water of welcome samarpayāmi I offer mukhe at his mouth ācamanīyam water for sipping samarpayāmi I offer

Salutation to the great lord of the hosts: I meditate on you, invoke you, offer a jewelled throne, water for the feet, water for the hands, and water to sip.

āpo hi ṣṭhā mayobhuvastā na ūrje dadhātana mahe raṇāya cakṣase |
yo vaśśivatamo rasastasya bhājayateha naḥ uśatīriva mātaraḥ |
tasmā araṃ gamāma vo yasya kṣayāya jinvatha āpo janayathā ca naḥ ||
śrīmahāgaṇādhipataye namaḥ snapayāmi | snānānantaraṃ śuddhācamanīyaṃ samarpayāmi ||

word by word · translation

āpaḥ O waters hi indeed sthā you are mayaḥ-bhuvaḥ bringers of joy tāḥ as such naḥ us ūrje to strength dadhātana set mahe to great raṇāya delight cakṣase and to vision yaḥ which vaḥ your śiva-tamaḥ most auspicious rasaḥ essence tasya of that bhājayata give a share iha here naḥ to us uśatīḥ loving iva like mātaraḥ mothers tasmai to him aram readily gamāma may we go vaḥ for you yasya whose kṣayāya abode jinvatha you quicken āpaḥ O waters janayatha you bring forth ca and naḥ us śrī-mahā-gaṇa-adhipataye to the great Lord of hosts namaḥ salutation snapayāmi I bathe him snāna-anantaram after the bath śuddha-ācamanīyam pure water for sipping samarpayāmi I offer

O waters, you are the source of well-being: set us in strength, to see great joy. Your most blessed essence — let us share in it here, like loving mothers. To you we come gladly, for whose dwelling you quicken us; waters, give us birth. — I bathe him; after the bath I offer pure water to sip.

abhi vastrā suvasanānyarṣābhi dhenūssudughāḥ pūyamānaḥ |
abhi candrā bhartave no hiraṇyābhyaśvānrathino deva soma ||
śrīmahāgaṇādhipataye namaḥ vastrayugmaṃ samarpayāmi ||

word by word · translation

abhi towards vastrā garments su-vasanāni fine to wear arṣa flow abhi towards dhenūḥ cows su-dughāḥ rich in milk pūyamānaḥ as you are purified abhi towards candrā shining gold bhartave to bear naḥ for us hiraṇyā golden things abhi towards aśvān horses rathinaḥ with chariots deva O god soma Soma śrī-mahā-gaṇa-adhipataye to the great Lord of hosts namaḥ salutation vastra-yugmam a pair of garments samarpayāmi I offer

Flowing clear, O god Soma, bring us garments, fine clothing, and cows that yield milk readily; bring us shining gold to keep, and horses with chariots. — I offer a pair of garments.

yajñopavītaṃ paramaṃ pavitraṃ prajāpateryatsahajaṃ purastāt |
āyuṣyamagryaṃ pratimuñca śubhraṃ yajñopavītaṃ balamastu tejaḥ ||
śrīmahāgaṇādhipataye namaḥ yajñopavītaṃ samarpayāmi ||

word by word · translation

yajña-upavītam the sacred thread paramam the supreme pavitram purifier prajā-pateḥ with Prajāpati yat which saha-jam was born purastāt of old āyuṣyam giving long life agryam foremost pratimuñca put on śubhram bright yajña-upavītam the sacred thread balam strength astu may it be tejaḥ and splendour śrī-mahā-gaṇa-adhipataye to the great Lord of hosts namaḥ salutation yajña-upavītam the sacred thread samarpayāmi I offer

The sacred thread, supremely pure, born of old together with Prajāpati — put on this bright thread, foremost giver of long life; may it be strength and splendour. — I offer the sacred thread.

gandhadvārāṃ durādharṣāṃ nityapuṣṭāṃ karīṣiṇīm |
īśvarīgṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ tāmihopahvaye śriyam ||
śrīmahāgaṇādhipataye namaḥ divyaśrīcandanaṃ samarpayāmi ||

word by word · translation

gandha-dvārām known by her fragrance durādharṣām unassailable nitya-puṣṭām ever abundant karīṣiṇīm rich in the earth's bounty īśvarīm the sovereign sarva-bhūtānām of all beings tām her iha here upahvaye I call śriyam Śrī śrī-mahā-gaṇa-adhipataye to the great Lord of hosts namaḥ salutation divya-śrī-candanam divine sandal paste samarpayāmi I offer

Her whose door is fragrance, unassailable, ever-nourishing, rich in harvest, sovereign over all beings — Śrī I call here. — I offer divine sandal.

āyane te parāyaṇe dūrvā rohantu puṣpiṇīḥ |
hradāśca puṇḍarīkāṇi samudrasya gṛhā ime ||
śrīmahāgaṇādhipataye namaḥ dūrvādinānāvidhapuṣpāṇi pūjayāmi ||

word by word · translation

āyane at your coming te your parāyaṇe and going dūrvāḥ dūrvā grass rohantu may it grow puṣpiṇīḥ flowering hradāḥ pools ca and puṇḍarīkāṇi white lotuses samudrasya of the ocean gṛhāḥ are homes ime these śrī-mahā-gaṇa-adhipataye to the great Lord of hosts namaḥ salutation dūrvā-ādi-nānā-vidha-puṣpāṇi dūrvā grass and flowers of many kinds pūjayāmi I offer in worship

Where you come and where you go, may flowering dūrvā grow, and ponds with lotuses: these are the ocean's homes. — I worship with dūrvā and many flowers.

oṃ sumukhāya namaḥ
oṃ ekadantāya namaḥ
oṃ kapilāya namaḥ
oṃ gajakarṇakāya namaḥ
oṃ lambodarāya namaḥ
oṃ vikaṭāya namaḥ
oṃ vighnarājāya namaḥ
oṃ gaṇādhipāya namaḥ
oṃ dhūmaketave namaḥ
oṃ gaṇādhyakṣāya namaḥ
oṃ phālacandrāya namaḥ
oṃ gajānanāya namaḥ
oṃ vakratuṇḍāya namaḥ
oṃ śūrpakarṇāya namaḥ
oṃ herambāya namaḥ
oṃ skandapūrvajāya namaḥ
oṃ sarvasiddhipradāyakāya namaḥ
oṃ śrīmahāgaṇādhipataye namaḥ

word by word · translation

om su-mukhāya namaḥ om, salutation to him of the fair face om eka-dantāya namaḥ om, salutation to the one-tusked om kapilāya namaḥ om, salutation to the tawny one om gaja-karṇakāya namaḥ om, salutation to the elephant-eared om lamba-udarāya namaḥ om, salutation to the large-bellied om vikaṭāya namaḥ om, salutation to the mighty om vighna-rājāya namaḥ om, salutation to the king of obstacles om gaṇa-adhipāya namaḥ om, salutation to the lord of hosts om dhūma-ketave namaḥ om, salutation to the smoke-bannered om gaṇa-adhyakṣāya namaḥ om, salutation to the overseer of hosts om phāla-candrāya namaḥ om, salutation to him with the moon on his brow om gaja-ānanāya namaḥ om, salutation to the elephant-faced om vakra-tuṇḍāya namaḥ om, salutation to him of the curved trunk om śūrpa-karṇāya namaḥ om, salutation to him with ears like winnowing fans om herambāya namaḥ om, salutation to Heramba om skanda-pūrva-jāya namaḥ om, salutation to the elder brother of Skanda om sarva-siddhi-pradāyakāya namaḥ om, salutation to the giver of all success om śrī-mahā-gaṇa-adhipataye namaḥ om, salutation to the great Lord of hosts

Ṣoḍaśa-nāma pūjā — the sixteen names: the fair-faced, the one-tusked, the tawny, the elephant-eared, the pot-bellied, the formidable, king of obstacles, lord of the hosts, the smoke-bannered, chief of the hosts, moon-browed, elephant-faced, curved-trunked, winnow-eared, Heramba, elder brother of Skanda; giver of every success; the great lord of the hosts. — Then: many fragrant flowers are offered.

vanaspatyudbhavairdivyairnānāgandhaissusaṃyutaḥ |
āghreyassarvadevānāṃ dhūpo'yaṃ pratigṛhyatām ||
śrīmahāgaṇādhipataye namaḥ dhūpamāghrāpayāmi ||

word by word · translation

vanaspati-udbhavaiḥ that come from trees divyaiḥ divine nānā-gandhaiḥ with many fragrances su-saṃyutaḥ well blended āghreyaḥ fit to be smelt sarva-devānām by all the gods dhūpaḥ incense ayam this pratigṛhyatām may it be accepted śrī-mahā-gaṇa-adhipataye to the great Lord of hosts namaḥ salutation dhūpam incense āghrāpayāmi I offer to be smelt

Made of heavenly things sprung from the trees, rich with many scents, fit to be smelled by all the gods — accept this incense. — I offer incense.

sājyaṃ trivartisaṃyuktaṃ vahninā yojitaṃ priyam |
gṛhāṇa maṅgalaṃ dīpaṃ trailokyatimirāpaham ||
bhaktyā dīpaṃ prayacchāmi devāya paramātmane |
trāhi māṃ narakādghorāddivyajyotirnamo'stu te ||
śrīmahāgaṇādhipataye namaḥ dīpaṃ darśayāmi | dhūpadīpānantaram ācamanīyaṃ samarpayāmi ||

word by word · translation

sa-ājyam with ghee tri-varti-saṃyuktam with three wicks vahninā with fire yojitam lit priyam pleasing gṛhāṇa accept maṅgalam auspicious dīpam lamp trailokya-timira-apaham that removes the darkness of the three worlds bhaktyā with devotion dīpam the lamp prayacchāmi I offer devāya to the god parama-ātmane the supreme Self trāhi save mām me narakāt from hell ghorāt terrible divya-jyotiḥ O divine light namaḥ salutation astu be te to you śrī-mahā-gaṇa-adhipataye to the great Lord of hosts namaḥ salutation dīpam the lamp darśayāmi I show dhūpa-dīpa-anantaram after incense and lamp ācamanīyam water for sipping samarpayāmi I offer

With ghee and a triple wick, lit with fire, dear to you — accept this auspicious lamp that drives away the darkness of the three worlds. With devotion I offer the lamp to the god, the supreme Self: save me from dreadful hell; salutation to you, divine light. — I show the lamp; after incense and lamp, I offer water to sip.

Naivedya: place a piece of jaggery before him and sprinkle water around it while reciting. (At night say ṛtaṃ tvā satyena pariṣiñcāmi instead.)

oṃ bhūrbhuvassuvaḥ | oṃ tatsaviturvareṇyaṃ bhargo devasya dhīmahi | dhiyo yo naḥ pracodayāt ||
satyaṃ tvartena pariṣiñcāmi |
śrīmahāgaṇādhipataye namaḥ guḍopahāranaivedyaṃ samarpayāmi |
amṛtamastu | amṛtopastaraṇamasi |
oṃ prāṇāya svāhā | oṃ apānāya svāhā | oṃ vyānāya svāhā |
oṃ udānāya svāhā | oṃ samānāya svāhā |
madhye madhye pānīyaṃ samarpayāmi | amṛtāpidhānamasi | uttarāpośanaṃ samarpayāmi |
hastau prakṣālayāmi | pādau prakṣālayāmi | śuddhācamanīyaṃ samarpayāmi ||

word by word · translation

om bhūḥ bhuvaḥ suvaḥ om, earth, mid-region, heaven om om tat that savituḥ of Savitṛ vareṇyam most excellent bhargaḥ radiance devasya of the god dhīmahi we meditate on dhiyaḥ thoughts yaḥ who naḥ our pracodayāt may impel satyam you, truth tvā you ṛtena with order pariṣiñcāmi I sprinkle around śrī-mahā-gaṇa-adhipataye to the great Lord of hosts namaḥ salutation guḍa-upahāra-naivedyam an offering of jaggery samarpayāmi I offer amṛtam nectar astu may it be amṛta-upastaraṇam the under-layer of nectar asi you are om prāṇāya svāhā om, hail to the in-breath om apānāya svāhā om, hail to the out-breath om vyānāya svāhā om, hail to the diffused breath om udānāya svāhā om, hail to the up-breath om samānāya svāhā om, hail to the equalising breath madhye madhye in between pānīyam drinking water samarpayāmi I offer amṛta-apidhānam the covering of nectar asi you are uttara-āpośanam the closing sip samarpayāmi I offer hastau the hands prakṣālayāmi I wash pādau the feet prakṣālayāmi I wash śuddha-ācamanīyam pure water for sipping samarpayāmi I offer

After the Gāyatrī: I sprinkle you, truth, with order. I offer an offering of jaggery. May it be nectar; you are the nectar-spread beneath. Hail to the five vital breaths. I offer water to drink between mouthfuls; you are the nectar-covering; I offer the closing sip; I wash the hands and feet, and offer pure water to sip.

pūgīphalaissakarpūrairnāgavallīdalairyutam |
muktācūrṇena saṃyuktaṃ tāmbūlaṃ pratigṛhyatām ||
śrīmahāgaṇādhipataye namaḥ tāmbūlaṃ samarpayāmi ||

word by word · translation

pūgī-phalaiḥ with areca nuts sa-karpūraiḥ and camphor nāga-vallī-dalaiḥ with betel leaves yutam joined muktā-cūrṇena with pearl-lime saṃyuktam combined tāmbūlam betel pratigṛhyatām may it be accepted śrī-mahā-gaṇa-adhipataye to the great Lord of hosts namaḥ salutation tāmbūlam betel samarpayāmi I offer

Accept this betel — areca nut with camphor, betel leaves, and lime of pearl. — I offer betel.

gaṇānāṃ tvā gaṇapatigṃ havāmahe kaviṃ kavīnāmupamaśravastamam |
jyeṣṭharājaṃ brahmaṇāṃ brahmaṇaspata ā naśśṛṇvannūtibhissīda sādanam ||
śrīmahāgaṇādhipataye namaḥ suvarṇamantrapuṣpaṃ samarpayāmi ||

word by word · translation

gaṇānām of the hosts tvā you gaṇa-patim the lord of hosts havāmahe we invoke kavim the seer kavīnām of seers upama-śravaḥ-tamam of highest renown jyeṣṭha-rājam the eldest king brahmaṇām of sacred words brahmaṇaḥ-pate O lord of the sacred word ā hither naḥ us śṛṇvan hearing ūtibhiḥ with your help sīda sit down sādanam on the seat śrī-mahā-gaṇa-adhipataye to the great Lord of hosts namaḥ salutation suvarṇa-mantra-puṣpam the golden flower of mantra samarpayāmi I offer

We call on you, lord of the hosts … sit in your seat. — I offer the golden flower of mantra.

mantrahīnaṃ kriyāhīnaṃ bhaktihīnaṃ gaṇādhipa |
yatpūjitaṃ mayā deva paripūrṇaṃ tadastu te ||

word by word · translation

mantra-hīnam lacking in mantra kriyā-hīnam lacking in rite bhakti-hīnam lacking in devotion gaṇa-adhipa O lord of hosts yat whatever pūjitam worship has been done mayā by me deva O god paripūrṇam complete tat that astu may it be te for you

Lacking in mantra, lacking in rite, lacking in devotion — whatever worship I have done, O lord of the hosts, may it be made complete for you.

anayā dhyānāvāhanādiṣoḍaśopacārapūjayā ca bhagavān sarvātmakaḥ
śrīmahāgaṇādhipatissuprīto varado bhūtvā
uttare karmaṇyavighnamastviti bhavanto bruvantu |
uttare karmaṇyavighnamastu |
gaṇādhipatiprasādaṃ śirasā gṛhṇāmi ||

word by word · translation

anayā by this dhyāna-āvāhana-ādi-ṣoḍaśa-upacāra-pūjayā worship of sixteen services, beginning with meditation and invocation ca and bhagavān the blessed sarva-ātmakaḥ Self of all śrī-mahā-gaṇa-adhipatiḥ great Lord of hosts su-prītaḥ well pleased vara-daḥ and granting boons bhūtvā becoming uttare in the following karmaṇi rite avighnam freedom from obstacles astu may there be iti thus bhavantaḥ you, sirs bruvantu please say uttare in the following karmaṇi rite avighnam freedom from obstacles astu may there be gaṇa-adhipati-prasādam the grace of the Lord of hosts śirasā on my head gṛhṇāmi I receive

By this sixteen-fold worship may the Lord, the Self of all, the great lord of the hosts, be well pleased and grant boons. — Let the assembly say: may the rite that follows be free of obstacles. (They answer:) May it be free of obstacles. I receive Gaṇapati's grace upon my head.

sahasraparamā devī śatamūlā śatāṅkurā |
sarvagṃ haratu me pāpaṃ dūrvā duḥsvapnanāśanī ||
gaṇapatiṃ yathāsthānamudvāsayāmi ||

word by word · translation

sahasra-paramā supreme among a thousand devī the goddess śata-mūlā of a hundred roots śata-aṅkurā of a hundred shoots sarvam all haratu may she take away me my pāpam sin dūrvā Dūrvā duḥsvapna-nāśanī destroyer of bad dreams gaṇa-patim Gaṇapati yathā-sthānam to his own place udvāsayāmi I give leave to depart

The goddess Dūrvā, of a thousand shoots, a hundred roots and a hundred sprouts, destroyer of evil dreams — may she take away all my sin. I bid Gaṇapati return to his place.

yajñena yajñamayajanta devāstāni dharmāṇi prathamānyāsan |
te ha nākaṃ mahimānassacante yatra pūrve sādhyāssanti devāḥ ||

word by word · translation

yajñena with the sacrifice yajñam the sacrifice ayajanta sacrificed devāḥ the gods tāni those dharmāṇi ordinances prathamāni the first āsan were te those ha indeed nākam heaven mahimānaḥ mighty ones sacante reach yatra where pūrve the ancient sādhyāḥ Sādhyas santi dwell devāḥ the gods

With sacrifice the gods worshipped the Sacrifice; these were the first ordinances. Those great ones reached the vault of heaven, where the ancient Sādhyas, the gods, abide.

❧

Pañcalokapāla Pūjā · the five world-guardians ↑

ācamya | pūrvokta evaṃguṇaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭāyāṃ śubhatithau
śrīsatyanārāyaṇavratāṅgagaṇapatyādipañcalokapālakapūjāṃ
ādityādinavagrahapūjām indrādyaṣṭadikpālakapūjāṃ ca kariṣye ||

word by word · translation

ācamya having sipped water pūrva-ukte as stated before evam-guṇa-viśeṣaṇa-viśiṣṭāyām distinguished by such qualities śubha-tithau on this auspicious day śrī-satyanārāyaṇa-vrata-aṅga-gaṇapati-ādi-pañca-loka-pālaka-pūjām the worship of the five guardians of the world, Gaṇapati and the rest, as part of the Satyanārāyaṇa vow āditya-ādi-nava-graha-pūjām the worship of the nine planets, the Sun and the rest indra-ādi-aṣṭa-dik-pālaka-pūjām the worship of the eight guardians of the quarters, Indra and the rest ca and kariṣye I shall perform

Having sipped water: on this auspicious day of the qualities named before, as limbs of the Satyanārāyaṇa vrata I will worship the five world-guardians beginning with Gaṇapati, the nine planets beginning with the Sun, and the eight guardians of the quarters beginning with Indra.

oṃ gaṇānāṃ tvā gaṇapatigṃ havāmahe kaviṃ kavīnāmupamaśravastamam |
jyeṣṭharājaṃ brahmaṇāṃ brahmaṇaspata ā naśśṛṇvannūtibhissīda sādanam ||
sāṅgaṃ sāyudhaṃ savāhanaṃ saśaktiṃ patnīputraparivārasametaṃ gaṇapatiṃ lokapālakamāvāhayāmi sthāpayāmi pūjayāmi ||

word by word · translation

om om gaṇānām of the hosts tvā you gaṇa-patim the lord of hosts havāmahe we invoke kavim the seer kavīnām of seers upama-śravaḥ-tamam of highest renown jyeṣṭha-rājam the eldest king brahmaṇām of sacred words brahmaṇaḥ-pate O lord of the sacred word ā hither naḥ us śṛṇvan hearing ūtibhiḥ with your help sīda sit down sādanam on the seat sa-aṅgam with his limbs sa-āyudham his weapons sa-vāhanam his mount sa-śaktim his power patnī-putra-parivāra-sametam and with wife, sons and retinue gaṇa-patim Gaṇapati loka-pālakam guardian of the world āvāhayāmi I invoke sthāpayāmi I install pūjayāmi I worship

1. Gaṇapati: “We call on you, lord of the hosts …” — with his limbs, weapons, mount and power, with consort, sons and retinue, I invoke, install and worship Gaṇapati as world-guardian.

oṃ brahmā devānāṃ padavīḥ kavīnāmṛṣirviprāṇāṃ mahiṣo mṛgāṇām |
śyeno gṛdhrāṇāgṃ svadhitirvanānāgṃ somaḥ pavitramatyeti rebhan ||
sāṅgaṃ sāyudhaṃ savāhanaṃ saśaktiṃ patnīputraparivārasametaṃ brahmāṇaṃ lokapālakamāvāhayāmi sthāpayāmi pūjayāmi ||

word by word · translation

om om brahmā the priest devānām of the gods pada-vīḥ the leader kavīnām of poets ṛṣiḥ the seer viprāṇām of the inspired mahiṣaḥ the buffalo mṛgāṇām of beasts śyenaḥ the falcon gṛdhrāṇām of birds of prey sva-dhitiḥ the axe vanānām of forests somaḥ Soma pavitram the strainer atyeti passes through rebhan singing sa-aṅgam with his limbs sa-āyudham his weapons sa-vāhanam his mount sa-śaktim his power patnī-putra-parivāra-sametam and with wife, sons and retinue brahmāṇam Brahmā loka-pālakam guardian of the world āvāhayāmi I invoke sthāpayāmi I install pūjayāmi I worship

2. Brahmā: “Brahmā among the gods, leader among poets, seer among sages, buffalo among beasts, falcon among birds of prey, axe among the trees — Soma passes through the filter, singing.” I invoke, install and worship Brahmā as world-guardian.

oṃ idaṃ viṣṇurvicakrame tredhā nidadhe padam |
samūḍhamasya pāgṃsure ||
sāṅgaṃ sāyudhaṃ savāhanaṃ saśaktiṃ patnīputraparivārasametaṃ viṣṇuṃ lokapālakamāvāhayāmi sthāpayāmi pūjayāmi ||

word by word · translation

om om idam this viṣṇuḥ Viṣṇu vicakrame strode over tredhā thrice nidadhe he set down padam his foot samūḍham gathered up asya in his pāṃsure dusty footprint sa-aṅgam with his limbs sa-āyudham his weapons sa-vāhanam his mount sa-śaktim his power patnī-putra-parivāra-sametam and with wife, sons and retinue viṣṇum Viṣṇu loka-pālakam guardian of the world āvāhayāmi I invoke sthāpayāmi I install pūjayāmi I worship

3. Viṣṇu: “Viṣṇu strode over this; three times he set down his foot; all is gathered in his dusty footprint.” I invoke, install and worship Viṣṇu as world-guardian.

kadrudrāya pracetase mīḍhuṣṭamāya tavyase |
vocema śantamagṃ hṛde ||
sāṅgaṃ sāyudhaṃ savāhanaṃ saśaktiṃ patnīputraparivārasametaṃ rudraṃ lokapālakamāvāhayāmi sthāpayāmi pūjayāmi ||

word by word · translation

kat what rudrāya to Rudra pracetase the wise mīḍhuḥ-tamāya most bountiful tavyase the mightiest vocema may we speak śam-tamam most pleasing hṛde to his heart sa-aṅgam with his limbs sa-āyudham his weapons sa-vāhanam his mount sa-śaktim his power patnī-putra-parivāra-sametam and with wife, sons and retinue rudram Rudra loka-pālakam guardian of the world āvāhayāmi I invoke sthāpayāmi I install pūjayāmi I worship

4. Rudra: “What shall we say to Rudra, the wise, most bountiful, most mighty, that is dearest to his heart?” I invoke, install and worship Rudra as world-guardian.

oṃ gaurīrmimāya salilāni takṣatyekapadī dvipadī sā catuṣpadī |
aṣṭāpadī navapadī babhūvuṣī sahasrākṣarā parame vyoman ||
sāṅgāṃ sāyudhāṃ savāhanāṃ saśaktiṃ patiputraparivārasametāṃ
gaurīṃ lokapālikāmāvāhayāmi sthāpayāmi pūjayāmi ||

word by word · translation

om om gaurīḥ the buffalo-cow mimāya has lowed salilāni the waters takṣatī fashioning eka-padī one-footed dvi-padī two-footed sā she catuḥ-padī four-footed aṣṭā-padī eight-footed nava-padī nine-footed babhūvuṣī having become sahasra-akṣarā of a thousand syllables parame in the highest vyoman heaven sa-aṅgām with her limbs sa-āyudhām her weapons sa-vāhanām her mount sa-śaktim her power pati-putra-parivāra-sametām and with husband, sons and retinue gaurīm Gaurī loka-pālikām guardian of the world āvāhayāmi I invoke sthāpayāmi I install pūjayāmi I worship

5. Gaurī: “The buffalo-cow lowed, fashioning the floods — one-footed, two-footed, four-footed, eight-footed, nine-footed, of a thousand syllables in the highest heaven.” I invoke, install and worship Gaurī as world-guardian, with her consort, sons and retinue.

gaṇeśādipañcalokapālakadevatābhyo namaḥ |
dhyāyāmi āvāhayāmi ratnasiṃhāsanaṃ samarpayāmi pādyaṃ samarpayāmi
arghyaṃ samarpayāmi ācamanīyaṃ samarpayāmi snānaṃ samarpayāmi
śuddhācamanīyaṃ samarpayāmi vastraṃ samarpayāmi yajñopavītaṃ samarpayāmi
gandhaṃ samarpayāmi akṣatān samarpayāmi puṣpāṇi samarpayāmi
dhūpamāghrāpayāmi dīpaṃ darśayāmi naivedyaṃ samarpayāmi
tāmbūlaṃ samarpayāmi mantrapuṣpaṃ samarpayāmi ||
gaṇeśādipañcalokapālakadevatāprasādasiddhirastu ||

word by word · translation

gaṇeśa-ādi-pañca-loka-pālaka-devatābhyaḥ to the five guardian deities of the world, Gaṇeśa and the rest namaḥ salutation dhyāyāmi I meditate on them āvāhayāmi I invoke them ratna-siṃha-āsanam a jewelled lion-throne samarpayāmi I offer pādyam water for the feet samarpayāmi I offer arghyam water of welcome samarpayāmi I offer ācamanīyam water for sipping samarpayāmi I offer snānam a bath samarpayāmi I offer śuddha-ācamanīyam pure water for sipping samarpayāmi I offer vastram a garment samarpayāmi I offer yajña-upavītam the sacred thread samarpayāmi I offer gandham sandal paste samarpayāmi I offer akṣatān unbroken rice samarpayāmi I offer puṣpāṇi flowers samarpayāmi I offer dhūpam incense āghrāpayāmi I offer to be smelt dīpam a lamp darśayāmi I show naivedyam food samarpayāmi I offer tāmbūlam betel samarpayāmi I offer mantra-puṣpam the flower of mantra samarpayāmi I offer gaṇeśa-ādi-pañca-loka-pālaka-devatā-prasāda-siddhiḥ the gaining of the grace of the five guardian deities, Gaṇeśa and the rest astu may there be

Salutation to the five world-guardians led by Gaṇeśa: meditation, invocation, throne, water for feet and hands, water to sip, bath, pure water, garment, sacred thread, sandal, akṣata, flowers, incense, lamp, food, betel and the flower of mantra are offered. May the grace of the five world-guardians be gained.

❧

Navagraha Pūjā · the nine planets ↑

The planets are set out around the Sun, who stands near the vessel on its west side: the Moon to the south-east, Mars south, Mercury north-east, Jupiter north, Venus east, Saturn west, Rāhu south-west and Ketu north-west. Each is invoked with his presiding deity (adhidevatā) on his right and his counter-deity (pratyadhidevatā) on his left.

1 · Sūrya ↑

sūryāriṣṭe tu samprāpte sūryapūjāṃ ca kārayet |
sūryadhyānaṃ pravakṣyāmi ātmapīḍopaśāntaye ||

word by word · translation

sūrya-ariṣṭe when affliction from the Sun tu indeed samprāpte has come sūrya-pūjām the worship of the Sun ca then kārayet one should have performed sūrya-dhyānam the meditation on the Sun pravakṣyāmi I shall declare ātma-pīḍā-upaśāntaye for the calming of affliction of the self

When affliction from the Sun befalls, let the Sun be worshipped. I will tell the meditation on the Sun, to quiet suffering of the self.

ā satyenetyasya mantrasya hiraṇyastūpa ṛṣiḥ | savitā devatā | triṣṭup chandaḥ |
(mama) yajamānasyādhidevatāpratyadhidevatāsahitasūryagrahaprasādasiddhyarthe
sūryagrahārādhane viniyogaḥ ||

word by word · translation

ā satyena (the opening words) iti thus beginning asya of this mantrasya mantra hiraṇyastūpaḥ Hiraṇyastūpa ṛṣiḥ is the seer savitā Savitṛ devatā is the deity triṣṭup Triṣṭubh chandaḥ is the metre mama of me yajamānasya the sacrificer adhidevatā-pratyadhidevatā-sahita-sūrya-graha-prasāda-siddhi-arthe to gain the grace of the planet Sun with its presiding and co-presiding deities sūrya-graha-ārādhane in the worship of the planet Sun viniyogaḥ is its use

Of the mantra “ā satyena” the seer is Hiraṇyastūpa, the deity Savitṛ, the metre triṣṭubh; it is applied in the worship of the Sun, with his adhidevatā and pratyadhidevatā, to win his grace for the sacrificer.

vedīmadhye lalitakamale karṇikāyāṃ rathasthaḥ
saptāśvo'rko'ruṇarucivapuḥ saptarajjurdvibāhuḥ |
gotre ramye bahuvidhaguṇe kāśyapākhye prasūtaḥ
kāliṅgākhye viṣayajanitaḥ prāṅmukhaḥ padmahastaḥ ||
padmāsanaḥ padmakaro dvibāhuḥ padmadyutissaptaturaṅgavāhaḥ |
divākaro lokaguruḥ kirīṭī mayi prasādaṃ vidadhātu devaḥ ||

word by word · translation

vedī-madhye in the middle of the altar lalita-kamale on a lovely lotus karṇikāyām on its pericarp ratha-sthaḥ standing in his chariot sapta-aśvaḥ with seven horses arkaḥ the Sun aruṇa-ruci-vapuḥ his body of ruddy lustre sapta-rajjuḥ with seven reins dvi-bāhuḥ two-armed gotre in the lineage ramye lovely bahu-vidha-guṇe of manifold virtues kāśyapa-ākhye called Kāśyapa prasūtaḥ born kāliṅga-ākhye called Kaliṅga viṣaya-janitaḥ born in the country prāk-mukhaḥ facing east padma-hastaḥ lotus in hand padma-āsanaḥ seated on a lotus padma-karaḥ lotus-handed dvi-bāhuḥ two-armed padma-dyutiḥ lotus-bright sapta-turaṅga-vāhaḥ drawn by seven horses divā-karaḥ the maker of day loka-guruḥ teacher of the world kirīṭī crowned mayi on me prasādam grace vidadhātu may he bestow devaḥ the god

Dhyāna: in the middle of the altar, on the pericarp of a lovely lotus, stands the Sun in his chariot — seven horses, seven reins, two arms, body the red of dawn; born in the noble Kāśyapa line, sprung from the land of Kaliṅga, facing east, lotus in hand. Seated on a lotus, lotus in his hands, lotus-bright, drawn by seven steeds — may the Day-maker, teacher of the world, crowned, show me his grace.

ā satyena rajasā vartamāno niveśayannamṛtaṃ martyaṃ ca |
hiraṇyayena savitā rathenā devo yāti bhuvanā vipaśyan ||
oṃ bhūrbhuvassuvaḥ sūryagrahehāgaccha ||

word by word · translation

ā hither satyena with truth rajasā through the dark space vartamānaḥ moving niveśayan setting to rest amṛtam the immortal martyam the mortal ca and hiraṇyayena golden savitā Savitṛ rathena in his chariot ā hither devaḥ the god yāti comes bhuvanā the worlds vipaśyan surveying om om bhūḥ bhuvaḥ suvaḥ earth, mid-region, heaven sūrya-graha O planet Sun iha here āgaccha come

Moving through the realm of space by truth, laying to rest the immortal and the mortal, god Savitṛ comes in his golden chariot, beholding the worlds. — Come here, Sun.

sūryagrahaṃ raktavarṇaṃ raktagandhaṃ raktapuṣpaṃ raktamālyāmbaradharaṃ
raktacchatradhvajarathapatākādiśobhitaṃ divyarathasamārūḍhaṃ
meruṃ pradakṣiṇīkurvāṇaṃ prāṅmukhaṃ padmāsanasthaṃ dvibhujaṃ

patnīputraparivārasametaṃ grahamaṇḍale praviṣṭamasminnadhikaraṇe

word by word · translation

sūrya-graham the planet Sun rakta-varṇam red in colour rakta-gandham with red sandal paste rakta-puṣpam with red flowers rakta-mālya-ambara-dharam wearing red garlands and garments rakta-chatra-dhvaja-ratha-patākā-ādi-śobhitam adorned with red parasol, banner, chariot, flags and the rest divya-ratha-samārūḍham mounted on a divine chariot merum Mount Meru pradakṣiṇī-kurvāṇam circling to the right prāk-mukham facing east padma-āsana-stham seated on a lotus dvi-bhujam two-armed sapta-aśvam with seven horses sapta-rajjum with seven reins kaliṅga-deśa-adhipatim lord of the Kaliṅga country kāśyapa-sa-gotram of the Kāśyapa lineage prabhava-saṃvatsare in the year Prabhava māgha-māse in the month of Māgha śukla-pakṣe in the bright fortnight saptamyām on the seventh day bhānu-vāsare on Sunday aśvinī-nakṣatra-jātam born under the star Aśvinī siṃha-rāśi-adhipatim lord of the sign Leo kirīṭinam crowned sukha-āsīnam seated at ease patnī-putra-parivāra-sametam with wife, sons and retinue graha-maṇḍale the circle of planets praviṣṭam who has entered asmin on this adhikaraṇe base madhye in the centre vartula-ākāra-maṇḍale in a circular diagram sthāpita-svarṇa-pratimā-rūpeṇa in the form of the golden image set up sūrya-graham the planet Sun āvāhayāmi I invoke sthāpayāmi I install pūjayāmi I worship

The Sun — red of colour, with red sandal, red flowers, red garland and garments, adorned with red parasol, banner and pennant, mounted on his divine chariot, circling Meru sunwise, facing east, seated on a lotus, two-armed, with seven horses and seven reins, lord of Kaliṅga, of the Kāśyapa gotra, born in the year Prabhava, in Māgha, the bright fortnight, the seventh tithi, a Sunday, under Aśvinī, lord of Leo, crowned, seated at ease, with consort, sons and retinue — I invoke, install and worship in the golden image set in the circular maṇḍala at the centre of the planetary diagram.

oṃ agniṃ dūtaṃ vṛṇīmahe hotāraṃ viśvavedasam |
asya yajñasya sukratum ||
sūryagrahādhidevatām agniṃ sāṅgaṃ sāyudhaṃ savāhanaṃ saśaktiṃ patnīputraparivārasametaṃ

om om agnim Agni dūtam as messenger vṛṇīmahe we choose hotāram the invoker viśva-vedasam who knows all asya of this yajñasya sacrifice su-kratum the skilful one sūrya-graha-adhidevatām the presiding deity of the planet Sun agnim Agni sa-aṅgam with his limbs sa-āyudham his weapons sa-vāhanam his mount sa-śaktim his power patnī-putra-parivāra-sametam and with wife, sons and retinue sūrya-grahasya of the planet Sun dakṣiṇataḥ to the south agnim Agni āvāhayāmi I invoke sthāpayāmi I install pūjayāmi I worship

“We choose Agni as messenger, the invoker, knower of all, most skilful in this sacrifice.” Agni, presiding deity of the Sun, is invoked on the Sun's right.

oṃ kadrudrāya pracetase mīḍhuṣṭamāya tavyase |
vocema śantamagṃ hṛde ||

word by word · translation

om om kat what rudrāya to Rudra pracetase the wise mīḍhuḥ-tamāya most bountiful tavyase the mightiest vocema may we speak śam-tamam most pleasing hṛde to his heart sūrya-graha-pratyadhidevatām the co-presiding deity of the planet Sun rudram Rudra sa-aṅgam with his limbs sa-āyudham his weapons sa-vāhanam his mount sa-śaktim his power patnī-putra-parivāra-sametam and with wife, sons and retinue sūrya-grahasya of the planet Sun uttarataḥ to the north rudram Rudra āvāhayāmi I invoke sthāpayāmi I install pūjayāmi I worship

“What shall we say to Rudra … dearest to his heart?” Rudra, counter-deity of the Sun, is invoked on the Sun's left.

2 · Candra ↑

candrāriṣṭe tu samprāpte candrapūjāṃ ca kārayet |
candradhyānaṃ pravakṣyāmi manaḥpīḍopaśāntaye ||

word by word · translation

candra-ariṣṭe when affliction from the Moon tu indeed samprāpte has come candra-pūjām the worship of the Moon ca then kārayet one should have performed candra-dhyānam the meditation on the Moon pravakṣyāmi I shall declare manaḥ-pīḍā-upaśāntaye for the calming of affliction of the mind

When affliction from the Moon befalls, let the Moon be worshipped. I will tell the meditation on the Moon, to quiet suffering of the mind.

āpyāyasvetyasya mantrasya gautama ṛṣiḥ | candro devatā | gāyatrī chandaḥ |

āpyāyasva (the opening words) iti thus beginning asya of this mantrasya mantra gautamaḥ Gautama ṛṣiḥ is the seer candraḥ the Moon devatā is the deity gāyatrī Gāyatrī chandaḥ is the metre mama of me yajamānasya the sacrificer adhidevatā-pratyadhidevatā-sahita-candra-graha-prasāda-siddhi-arthe to gain the grace of the planet Moon with its presiding and co-presiding deities candra-graha-ārādhane in the worship of the planet Moon viniyogaḥ is its use

Of the mantra “āpyāyasva” the seer is Gautama, the deity the Moon, the metre gāyatrī; it is applied in the worship of the Moon.

āgneyabhāge saratho daśāśvaścātreyajo yāmunadeśajaśca |
pratyaṅmukhasthaścaturaśrapīṭhe gadādharāṅgo himavatsvabhāvaḥ ||
śvetāmbaraśśvetavapuḥ kirīṭī śvetadyutirdaṇḍadharo dvibāhuḥ |
candro'mṛtātmā varadaḥ kirīṭī śreyāṃsi mahyaṃ vidadhātu devaḥ ||

word by word · translation

āgneya-bhāge in the south-east quarter sa-rathaḥ with his chariot daśa-aśvaḥ of ten horses ca and ātreya-jaḥ born of the Ātreya line yāmuna-deśa-jaḥ born in the Yamunā country ca and pratyak-mukha-sthaḥ facing west catuḥ-aśra-pīṭhe on a square seat gadā-dhara-aṅgaḥ bearing a mace himavat-svabhāvaḥ cool as snow by nature śveta-ambaraḥ white-robed śveta-vapuḥ white-bodied kirīṭī crowned śveta-dyutiḥ of white radiance daṇḍa-dharaḥ bearing a staff dvi-bāhuḥ two-armed candraḥ the Moon amṛta-ātmā whose essence is nectar vara-daḥ giver of boons kirīṭī crowned śreyāṃsi blessings mahyam on me vidadhātu may he bestow devaḥ the god

Dhyāna: in the south-east, in his chariot of ten horses, born of Atri's line in the land of the Yamunā, facing west on a square seat, bearing a mace, cool as the snows. White-robed, white of body, crowned, white-shining, staff in hand, two-armed — may the Moon, whose self is nectar, giver of boons, bestow blessings on me.

oṃ āpyāyasva sametu te viśvatassoma vṛṣṇiyam |
bhavā vājasya saṅgathe ||
oṃ bhūrbhuvassuvaḥ candragrahehāgaccha ||

word by word · translation

om om āpyāyasva swell sametu may there gather te in you viśvataḥ from every side soma O Soma vṛṣṇiyam vigour bhava be vājasya of strength saṅgathe at the gathering om om bhūḥ bhuvaḥ suvaḥ earth, mid-region, heaven candra-graha O planet Moon iha here āgaccha come

“Swell, O Soma; let manly vigour gather to you from every side; be present where strength is won.” — Come here, Moon.

candragrahaṃ śvetavarṇaṃ śvetagandhaṃ śvetapuṣpaṃ śvetamālyāmbaradharaṃ
śvetacchatradhvajapatākādiśobhitaṃ divyarathasamārūḍhaṃ

candra-graham the planet Moon śveta-varṇam white in colour śveta-gandham with white sandal paste śveta-puṣpam with white flowers śveta-mālya-ambara-dharam wearing white garlands and garments śveta-chatra-dhvaja-patākā-ādi-śobhitam adorned with white parasol, banner, flags and the rest divya-ratha-samārūḍham mounted on a divine chariot merum Mount Meru pradakṣiṇī-kurvāṇam circling to the right daśa-aśva-ratha-vāhanam borne by a chariot of ten horses pratyak-mukham facing west dvi-bhujam two-armed daṇḍa-dharam bearing a staff yāmuna-deśa-adhipatim lord of the Yamunā country ātreya-sa-gotram of the Ātreya lineage saumya-saṃvatsare in the year Saumya kārtika-māse in the month of Kārtika śukla-pakṣe in the bright fortnight paurṇamāsyām on the full-moon day indu-vāsare on Monday kṛttikā-nakṣatra-jātam born under the star Kṛttikā karkaṭa-rāśi-adhipatim lord of the sign Cancer kirīṭinam crowned sukha-āsīnam seated at ease patnī-putra-parivāra-sametam with wife, sons and retinue graha-maṇḍale the circle of planets praviṣṭam who has entered asmin on this adhikaraṇe base sūrya-grahasya of the planet Sun āgneya-dik-bhāge in the south-east quarter sama-catuḥ-aśra-maṇḍale in a square diagram sthāpita-rajata-pratimā-rūpeṇa in the form of the silver image set up candra-graham the planet Moon āvāhayāmi I invoke sthāpayāmi I install pūjayāmi I worship

The Moon — white, with white sandal, flowers, garland and garments, white parasol and banner, in his divine ten-horsed chariot circling Meru, facing west, two-armed, holding a staff, lord of the Yamunā country, of Atri's gotra, born in the year Saumya, in Kārttika, on the full moon, a Monday, under Kṛttikā, lord of Cancer — I invoke in the silver image set in the square maṇḍala south-east of the Sun.

oṃ apsu me somo abravīdantarviśvāni bheṣajā |
agniṃ ca viśvaśambhuvamāpaśca viśvabheṣajīḥ ||
candragrahādhidevatāḥ sāṅgāḥ sāyudhāḥ savāhanāḥ saśaktīḥ putraparivārasametāḥ
